```
import numpy as np, time, sys
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len  # or numpy

def make_nfft(m): return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def normalize_positive(a, floor=1e-12):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a[~np.isfinite]=0
    a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 0.01: a=np.ones_like(a)*1e-9
    a=a/s
    a=np.maximum(a, floor*1e-6?) 
    a/=sum
    return a

def conv_square(a):
    n=a.size
    N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
    F=rfft(a,N)
    q=irfft(F*F, N)
    q=q[:2*n-1]
    q[np.abs(q)<1e-14]=0
    q=np.maximum(q,0)
    # Due to roundoff sum maybe not 1; can rescale? For score, q sum should 1. Normalize to sum1? If normalized a, sum q=1; slight error. q *= 1/q.sum() if sum>0. This also corrects any scaling if a not normalized? We'll normalize a inside.
    return q

def score_from_q(q,n): return 2*n*np.max(q)

def correlate_weights(p,w):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    N=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length() maybe.
    v=w[::-1]
    P=rfft(p,N); V=rfft(v,N)
    c=irfft(V*P,N)
    if n==1: return np.array([c[0]?]) but not.
    S=c[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]
    S=np.maximum(S,0)
    return S
```
Check slice length: for n=4 L=7, c length? V len L, W len n -> full length L+n-1=10? indices 0..9. Need idx 6,5,4,3. `L-n-1=7-4-1=2`, slice `6:2:-1` gives 6->3 length4. Good. For n=2 L=3, c length 4, slice start2, stop 3-2-1=0, step -1 gives 2,1 length2. Desired i0 c[2], i1 c[1]. OK. For n=1 L=1, slice 0:-1:-1? returns length1? Python 0:-1:-1 gives [0]? yes maybe. Weights:
```
def softmax_weights(q, gamma):
    # gamma relative
    m=np.max(q)
    if m<=0: return np.full(q.shape,1/q.size)
    z=gamma*(q-m)
    z-=np.max(z)
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if not finite or s==0: w=np.zeros; active where q>=m...?
    return w/s
```
Gamma is exponent range. If gamma=10, edge exponents -10, weights sum ~e^10/(e^10-1) etc.

Multiplicative run:
```
def run_local_search(p0, n, time_limit, initial_score=None, gamma_seq=(5,15,50,150), lam0=0.1, noise=0.01, seed=...):
   p=normalize_positive(p0)
   q=conv_square(p)
   cur=2*n*max(q)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
   lam=lam0
   no_improve=0
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   while time < limit:
      # choose gamma maybe schedule
      gamma = current
      # compute weights
      w=softmax_weights(q, gamma)
      S=correlate_weights(p,w)
      sc = S*n
      sc -= mean(sc)
      # optionally clip extremes
      sc = np.clip(sc, -5, 5)
      newp=p*np.exp(-lam*sc)
      if noise>0:
          newp *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,noise,size=n))
      newp=np.maximum(newp,0)
      if newp.sum() <=0: revert
      newp/=newp.sum()
      newq=conv_square(newp)
      newsc=2*n*max(newq)
      if newsc < cur:
          p=newp; q=newq; cur=newsc
          if cur < best_score:
              best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
              no_improve=0
              # maybe increase lam
          else no_improve++
          if best_score <= target: return best
      else:
          # reduce lam
          lam *= 0.8
          # accept with probability small if noise? maybe if no_improve large use random restart
          no_improve+=1
      # adapt gamma? if no_improve>... gamma changes, lam increase
```
This is monotone; if stuck, no improvement. Need escape. We can after X iterations perform a random "kick": mix with random smooth or revert to best and restart with new gamma. Since global best from best; current may stagnate. Keep local best. If no improve for say 500 iters, do `p = best.copy()` then add noise/mix with random init, reset lam. This is basin hopping.

But monotone local search may be slow if step too small. We can use simulated annealing: accept worse with prob exp(-(newsc-cur)/T); update p=newp regardless. This can escape but score worsens. Implement scoring details:
```
def conv_square_normalized(a):
    a = safe_normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    N = 1
    while N < 2*n - 1: N <<=1
    F = rfft(a, N)
    q = irfft(F * F, N)
    q = q[:2*n-1]
    s = q.sum()
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s): q = q / s  # enforce sum=1? Might adjust numerical.
    q[q < 0] = 0
    return q
```
Dividing q by sum can compensate if a not exactly sum1. But if a normalized, sum q should 1. This scaling could hide issues if some q negative large? It also changes max relative; but numerical only. For correctness, if we normalize a to sum1, q sum exactly 1 mathematically; dividing by `s` is okay. But if due to FFT roundoff s=0.999999, good. If a had clipping floor, sum1. Use `q = np.maximum(q,0); s=q.sum(); if s>0: q/=s`. This ensures score=2n maxq. If q sum not 1 due to roundoff, adjust. But if many negative clipped, sum less; scaling might overestimate? Negatives small. Good.

However, if we use q normalized by sum for weights, but a values are sum1. Fine.

For exact final evaluation, we will call `evaluate_sequence` on candidate list. If our best score < target but exact is worse due to our normalization/division? Safe normalize:
```
def safe_normalize(a, floor=1e-12):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float)
   if a.ndim!=1: a=a.ravel()
   a[~np.isfinite]=0
   a=np.where(a>0,a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 0: a=np.ones_like(a)*1.0
   else: a=a/s
   a = np.maximum(a, floor)
   a = a/a.sum()
   return a
```
Floor relative? floor=1e-12 may too small; for n=4096 total floor 4e-9 negligible. Good. But if some entries become 0, gradient S may leave them zero; noise and final mix with uniform can reintroduce. In update, after normalization, ensure `p = np.maximum(p, floor)` and renormalize.

`weighted_correlate(p,w)`: Need ensure w nonnegative sum1. Use FFT power of two.
```
def weighted_correlate(p,w):
    n=p.size; L=2*n-1
    total = L + n -1 # 3n-2
    N=1
    while N < total: N <<=1
    v = w[::-1]
    P = rfft(p, N)
    V = rfft(v, N)
    c = irfft(V*P, N)
    c = c.real
    if n == 1:
       return np.array([float(sum(w)*p[0])])? Not needed.
    start=L-1
    stop=L-n-1
    S=c[start:stop:-1]
    S=np.maximum(S,0)
    return S
```
Need ensure c length at least N. For n=2, total=4, N=4, L=3, slice 2:0:-1 -> [2,1]. Good.
Check correctness with tiny example maybe: p=[a,b,c], w length5. S0=a w0 + b w1 + c w2. conv(v,p): v=[w4,w3,w2,w1,w0]. c[4]=v0p4? p padded zeros? Let's compute formula: full conv v (len5) and p(len3), c[m]=∑_{u=0}^{4} v[u]p[m-u]. For m=4 (L-1=4): p indices valid m-u 0..2 => u=4,3,2: v4= w0 * p0 + v3=w1*p1+v2=w2*p2. Good. m=3: u=3,2,1: v3=w1 p0 + v2=w2 p1 + v1=w3 p2. S1 desired a w1+b w2+c w3. Good. m=2: u=2,1,0: w2p0+w3p1+w4p2. Yes. Slice correct.

Multiplicative update function:
```
def multiplicative_step(p, q, penalty, mu, noise, rng):
    n=p.size
    qmax=np.max(q)
    if qmax<=0:
       w=np.full(q.size,1/q.size)
    else:
       t=q/qmax
       w=np.power(t, penalty)
       s=w.sum()
       if not finite or s==0: w uniform top
       else w/=s
    S=weighted_correlate(p,w)
    avg=S.mean()
    if avg<=0: H=0
    else: H=S/avg -1
    # center by mean (already) but due clip
    H=np.mean? if mean not 0 after clip? We clip then mean: H=np.clip(H, -4,4); H-=H.mean()
    if noise:
       H = H + rng.normal(0, noise, size=n) # Add to log multiplier? Actually p*exp(-muH) + noise log; can combine H_noise.
    newp = p * np.exp(-mu * H)
    newp = np.maximum(newp, 0)
    s=newp.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None, False
    newp/=s
    newp=np.maximum(newp, floor)
    newp/=newp.sum()
    return newp, True
```
Maybe noise added to H with scale very small. `noise` as std of log; use `0.01`.

```
def run_mult_descent(p0, deadline, powers=(1,2,4), mu0=0.15, noise0=0.01, ...):
   p=safe_normalize(p0)
   q=conv_square(p)
   cur=2*n*np.max(q)
   best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
   mu=mu0; noise=noise0; power_idx=0
   no_improve=0
   last_print=0
   while time < deadline:
      # cycle powers: for each power do some iters or based on time
      power=powers[power_idx % len]
      newp,ok=multiplicative_step(p,q,power,mu,noise,rng)
      if not ok:
         mu*=0.8; noise*=0.5; continue
      newq=conv_square(newp)
      news=2*n*np.max(newq)
      if news < cur - 1e-12:
          p=newp; q=newq; cur=news
          if cur < best_score:
             best_score=cur; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
             # maybe update best global
          else no_improve+=1
          # adapt mu
          if no_improve < 50: mu=min(mu*1.05, 0.5)
          else: mu=0.15
      else:
          # try smaller mu up to 3
          accepted=False
          for _ in range(3):
             mu*=0.7
             newp2,ok2=...
             if ok2:
                newq2=...; news2=...
                if news2 < cur:
                   p=newp2; q=newq2; cur=news2; accepted=True
                   if cur<best...; break
          if not accepted:
             # reject, reduce mu/noise, stagnation
             mu*=0.8; noise*=0.5
             no_improve+=1
      # every K iters or no_improve>max: kick
      if no_improve > 300 or (iters_since_kick>1000):
          # restore best and mix with random/best
          p=best.copy()
          # random mix
          p = (1-mix)*p + mix*random_smooth(n, rng)
          p=safe_normalize(p)
          q=conv_square(p); cur=...
          no_improve=0; iters_since_kick=0
      # progress print
```

```
def penalty_weights(q, qmax, target_over, power):
   if target_over is None or target_over <=0:
       t=q/qmax; w=t**power
   else:
```

- `make_beta(p,q)`: x=(i+0.5)/n; xi = clip(x,1e-6,1-1e-6); a=np.power(xi,p)*np.power(1-xi,q); normalize.
- `make_poly(coeffs)`: a=np.exp(coeffs[0]+coeffs[1]*x+coeffs[2]*x*x). Need avoid overflow: coeffs moderate.
- `make_geometric(alpha)`: a=np.exp(-alpha*x); normalize; reverse: a[::-1]; also alpha positive.
- `make_random_smooth(n,rng)`: frequencies k=1..5, amplitudes uniform(0,1), phases; y=sum amp*cos(2πkx+phase); a=np.exp(y - max(y)); normalize. Add small uniform. This can create diverse.
- `resample_sequence(a,m)`: use np.interp. If a length 1? handle.
- `make_from_height`: if global exists and finite, clean. Maybe resample to n. Also maybe original length if we want separate run.

After generate, score at least top. To avoid storing too many, store top `max_starts=12`.

Now, local descent details need efficient line search. Let's implement a robust `descent_once`:
```
def descent_once(p0, deadline, target_score=1.5027, seed=1, max_no_improve=300, n=None):
    if p0 is None: return None, inf
    p = safe_normalize(p0 if n is None else resample(p0, n))
    q = conv_square(p)
    cur = score_from_q(q, p.size)
    best = p.copy(); best_score = cur
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    rng = default_rng(seed)
    mu = 0.15
    noise = 0.01
    since_improve=0
    total_iters=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # choose power based on elapsed fraction? Use per-call, not global. We can use total remaining vs start? Maintain internal iters.
        # Let's use power schedule by `total_iters`: first quarter power=1, second 2, third 4, later 8? But when many calls, alternate.
        power = [1.2, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0][min(3, total_iters//200)]  # maybe if each call short.
        # If this is a new call with few iters, use power=2? Could pass stage.
        # better: choose based on remaining: if elapsed/start >0.5 use higher.
        elapsed_frac = (time.monotonic()-start_time)/max(1e-6, deadline-start_time)
        if elapsed_frac <0.33: power=1.5
        elif elapsed_frac<0.66: power=2.5
        else: power=4.5
        ok = False
        for attempt in range(4):
            newp = do_step(p,q,power,target,p.size,mu,noise,rng)
            if newp is None: mu*=0.7; continue
            newq=conv_square(newp); news=score...
            if news < cur - 1e-12:
                p=newp; q=newq; cur=news; total_iters+=1; since_improve=0; ok=True
                if cur < best_score: best_score=cur; best=p.copy()
                if cur <= target_score*0.999: ... maybe break
                # adjust mu? if improvement, increase mu slightly
                if attempt > 0: mu = max(mu, 0.15)
                else: mu = min(0.4, mu*1.02)
                break
            else:
                mu *= 0.7
                noise *= 0.8
        if not ok:
            since_improve += 1
            # if no improve, occasionally accept a worse step with noise? Could help escape.
            # If since_improve > max_no_improve, restart
            if since_improve > max_no_improve:
                # restart: mix with best and random
                p = 0.7*best + 0.3*random_smooth(p.size, rng)
                p = safe_normalize(p)
                q=conv_square(p); cur=score...
                since_improve=0; mu=0.15; noise=0.05
            else:
                # try a random kick every 50 fails
                if since_improve % 50 == 0:
                    p = 0.9*best + 0.1*random_smooth(p.size, rng)
                    p=safe_normalize(p); q=...; cur=...
                    noise=min(0.2, noise*1.5)
        # print every 5s? maybe too much. Use if now-last_print>5: print score
    return best,best_score
```
This tries line search with decreasing mu if candidate worse. But if consistent no improve due to local min, it restarts.

Potential issue: If candidate score is slightly worse but due to numerical, accept threshold `-1e-12` maybe too strict. Use `news < cur * (1 - 1e-10)` or `news + 1e-12 < cur`. If not improve, reduce mu; may never accept small contributions. Use `improve_tol=1e-10`. OK.

Could include simulated acceptance: if `since_improve > 100`, accept worse with probability exp(-(news-cur)/T) where T=1e-5? Might help. But restarts enough.

`do_step` implementation:
```
def do_mstep(p,q,power,target,n,mu,noise,rng):
   qmax=q.max()
   if qmax<=0: return uniform
   wbase=(q/qmax)**power
   wtarget=None
   target_c=target/(2*n)
   if target_c < qmax:
      r=q-target_c
      r=r.where(r>0,0)
      rmax=r.max()
      if rmax>0:
         wtarget=(r/rmax)**power
   if wtarget is None:
      w=0.5*wbase +0.5/w.size? Actually combine:
   else:
      w=0.25*wbase +0.75*wtarget
   # add small uniform
   w = w + 1e-6
   s=w.sum(); if not finite or s==0: w uniform
   w/=s
   S=weighted_correlate(p,w)
   avg=S.mean()
   if avg <=0: H=zeros
   else: H=S/avg -1
   H=np.clip(H, -5,5)
   H=H-H.mean()
   if noise >0:
      H = H + rng.normal(0.0, noise, size=n)
      H=H-H.mean()
   newp=p*np.exp(-mu*H)
   # mix uniform
   rho=0.002
   newp=(1-rho)*newp + rho/n
   newp=safe_normalize(newp)
   return newp
```
Note: target_c independent of q scaling. If target score 1.5027, target_c=0.75135/n. If current score exactly 2, qmax=1/n=0.25? target_c=0.75/n. Good.
If target_c > qmax (already below target), wtarget none. Use qbase.

Power float: `np.power(q/qmax, power)`; if q has tiny negative? q clipped. For power >1, okay. To speed, use `t = q/qmax; wbase = t*t if power close?` Python.

Important: weights from target overshoot: r could be zero for all if qmax <= target_c; no. If target_c low relative, rpositive where q above target. For uniform, r positive for q>0.75/n (central ~50%); rmax=0.25/n? If wtarget normalized, it may greatly overshoot edges. Good.

If current q distribution has a huge spike, target residual weights on that spike, reducing it. Good.

Step H normalization: S/avg may be >1 due p variation. Clip H to [-5,5]. If p has zeros, S may zero for some, H negative large -> exp positive growing zeros? But p may be positive floor, S maybe zero if w support not overlapping? w length 2n-1, p positive floor, S positive. okay.

```
def spectral_iteration(a, M, n):
   N=next power
   A=rfft(a,N)
   c=irfft(A*A,N)
   c = c[:L]
   c=np.maximum(c,0)

   d=project_box_sum(c, M)
   C=rfft(d,N)
   # sqrt with principal:
   A2 = np.sqrt(C.astype(complex))
   # Force A2[0]=abs(A2[0])
   A2[0]=abs(A2[0])
   # If N even, A2[N//2] should be real maybe can choose sign? principal may imaginary if negative small; set real signed?

   a2=irfft(A2,N)
   a=float real : first n
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum(); if s>0: a=a/s else uniform
   return a
```

Implement `project_box_sum(c, M)`:
- Input c array length L, sum maybe s. Normalize to sum1? We should ensure sum=1. c from a sum1 should sum1; after clipping nonnegative maybe slightly. Do if s>0: c=c/s. If s<=0 uniform? But c sum should positive.

Simpler after binary, `d=np.clip(c+lam,0,M)`, sum may be 1 within tol; if not, adjust small. If max slightly >M due sum normalized, clip again and renormalize? Could loop 3. But small.
```
def project_box_sum(c, M):
   c=np.maximum(c,0)
   s=c.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(len(c),1/len(c))
   c=c/s
   L=c.size
   if M <= 1.0/(L-1)? use 1/L: M = max(M, 1.0/L*1.0001)
   if c.max() <= M:
      return c
   # binary lam
   lo=0.0
   hi=M - c.min()
   # ensure f(hi)>=1
   for _ in range(5):
      if box_sum(c,hi,M) >=1: break
      hi *= 2
      if hi > 10*M: break
   for 50 iters:
      mid=(lo+hi)/2
      if box_sum(c,mid,M) <1: lo=mid else hi=mid
   lam=(lo+hi)/2
   d=np.clip(c+lam,0,M)
   sd=d.sum(); if sd>0: d=d/sd
   # after renormalize max might >M if sd<1? But sd close. clip again
   d=np.clip(d,0,M)
   d/=d.sum() if sum
   return d
```
Box sum function top-level. Use `np.clip(c + lam, 0.0, M).sum()`.

Let's refine function `descent_once` to include both methods. Maybe have a `mode` parameter: 0=multiplicative,1=spectral. Main can allocate.
```
def run_spectral(a0, deadline, target_score, seed, max_relax=...):
    p=safe_normalize(a0)
    n=p.size; L=2n-1; N=next_fft_len(L)
    q=conv_square(p); cur=2*n*max(q)
    best=p, best_score=cur
    target_m=target_score/(2n)
    M=max(target_m, 0.95*cur_m?) current_m=cur/2n
    # ensure M >= average
    M=max(M, 1/(L)*1.01)
    no_improve=0
    damping=0.5?
    while time<deadline:
       p_new=spectral_step(p,M,n,N, damping)

          damping maybe increase?
```

```
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft

def next_fft_len(m):
    N=1
    while N < m: N <<=1
    return N

def _clean_sequence(a, floor=1e-12): ...
def normalize_sum1(a): ...

def _resample(a, m): ...

def conv_square(p):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1; N=next_fft_len(L)
   A=rfft(p,N)
   q=irfft(A*A,N)
   q=q[:L]
   q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
   s=q.sum()
   if s>0: q=q/s
   return q

def score_from_q(q,n): return float(2.0*n*np.max(q))
```

`spectral_step`:
```
def spectral_step(a, M, N, damp=0.7):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    A=rfft(a,N)
    Ccur=A*A
    c=irfft(Ccur,N)
    c=c[:L]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    s=c.sum()
    if s>0: c=c/s
    d=project_box_sum(c, M)
    Ctar=rfft(d,N)
    # Ctar complex; ensure Hermitian? rfft of real d.
    absC=np.abs(Ctar)
    r_target=np.sqrt(absC)
    absA=np.abs(A)
    # ratio phase
    # Ccur may zero. For phase_delta, use Ctar / (A*A + eps)
    denom=Ccur
    eps=1e-30
    denom_safe = denom + eps
    ratio = Ctar / denom_safe
    # phase_delta = np.angle(ratio) but if using safe, for |A| small phase noise.
    phase_delta=np.angle(ratio)
    # Alternatively target_phase=angle(Ctar); current_phase=angle(A); phase_delta=target_phase - 2 current_phase wrapped.
    # Use np.angle(np.exp(1j*(target -2current)))? Could.
    # For high robust, compute:
    target_phase=np.angle(Ctar)
    current_phase=np.angle(A)
    phase_delta=np.angle(np.exp(1j*(target_phase - 2.0*current_phase)))
    scale=r_target / np.maximum(absA, 1e-30)
    # cap scale to avoid explosions (but r_target and absA comparable if Ccur similar magnitude; target may differ)
    # Use median positive scale? median might be 1. cap ratio scale to [1e-6,1e6]
    scale=np.clip(scale,1e-6,1e6)
    A2=A * (scale * np.exp(1j*phase_delta))
    small=absA < 1e-12
    A2[small]=np.sqrt(Ctar[small])
    A2[0]=r_target[0] + 0j
    if N % 2 ==0:
       A2[N//2]=complex(r_target[N//2],0.0) # since need real nonnegative
    a2=irfft(A2,N)
    a2=np.maximum(a2[:n],0.0)
    # damping
    newa=(1.0-damp)*a + damp*a2
    newa=_clean(newa)
    return newa
```
Potential: The `phase_delta` using exp(wrapped) is okay. If target phase=current*2, delta 0, A2 magnitude target, phase current => exact target phase. If not, phase becomes target half. Good.
In Nyquist, if target Ctar real negative, `r_target` sqrt(abs) and phase pi; setting to positive may break. But target should after abs. We'll set `Ctar[N//2]=abs(Ctar[N//2].real)` before abs. Also if r_target[0] maybe 1.
`A2` length N//2+1 (rfft). `np.sqrt(Ctar[small])` uses complex principal; small array. Good.

But `irfft(A2,N)` expects A2 as complex rfft of size N//2+1. If A2 not satisfying conjugate? rfft format inherently for second half conj; values at 0 and Nyquist real. We set 0 real, Nyquist real. Good.

```
def run_spectral(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0, damp=0.7):
   rp=safe_normalize(a0)
   n=rp.size
   q=conv_square(rp); cur=2*n*max(q); m=cur/(2n)
   best=rp.copy(); best_score=cur
   L=2n-1; N=next_fft_len(L)
   target_m=target_score/(2n)
   avg=1.0/L
   M=max(target_m, avg*1.01)
   if m < M: M = max(avg*1.01, 0.5*m +0.5*M?) Actually if current already below target, use target.
   M=max(M, min(m, target_m*?))? If M > m, projection no effect? If M>=m, d=c (after sum) => no change. To make move, set M = min(m*0.95, max(target_m, avg*1.02)) if m>target_m else target_m*0.9? To reduce M even if target lower. 
   # initial M should be below current max to force descent:
   if m > target_m:
       M = max(target_m, 0.95*m)
   else:
       M = max(avg*1.01, 0.95*m)
   M=max(M, avg*1.01)
   no=0; it=0
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   while time < deadline:
       newa=spectral_step(rp, M, N, damp)
       q2=conv_square(newa); sc2=2*n*max(q2); m2=sc2/(2n)
       if sc2 < cur - 1e-12:
           rp=newa; cur=sc2; m=m2
           if cur<best_score: best=newa.copy(); best_score=cur
           no=0
           # tighten M a bit based on new max
           if m > target_m:
              M=max(target_m, 0.97*m)
           else:
              M=max(avg*1.01, 0.95*m)
           it+=1
       else:
           no+=1
           # if too low M, relax
           if no>10:
              M = min(m, max(M*1.05, target_m + 0.05*(m-target_m), avg*1.01))
              if M >= m: M = max(avg*1.01, 0.98*m)
              no=0
           if no>100 or it%? 
           # reset to best and reduce damp? 
           if no>150:
              rp=best.copy(); q=conv_square(rp); cur=...
              m=cur/(2n)
              M=max(avg*1.01, min(m*0.9? maybe 0.9*m, target_m?))
              no=0
       # print occasionally
   return best,best_score
```

Pseudo code details for `run_mult`:
```
def run_mult(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0, power_schedule=True):
    p=safe_normalize(a0)
    n=p.size
    q=conv_square(p); cur=score_from_q(q,n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    mu=0.15
    noise=0.02
    no_improve=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       elapsed = time.monotonic()-t0
       total = deadline-t0
       frac = elapsed/total if total>0 else 1
       if frac <0.3: power=1.5
       elif frac<0.6: power=2.5
       else: power=4.0
       ok=False
       for attempt in range(4):
           newp=mult_step(p,q,power,target_score,n,mu,noise,rng)
           if newp is None: mu*=0.7; noise*=0.7; continue
           newq=conv_square(newp)
           news=score_from_q(newq,n)
           if news < cur*(1-1e-9) or news < cur - 1e-12:
               p=newp; q=newq; cur=news; it+=1; no_improve=0; ok=True
               if cur<best_score: best_score=cur; best=p.copy()
               if attempt>0: mu=max(mu,0.15)
               else: mu=min(0.35, mu*1.03)
               noise=min(0.05, noise*1.02)
               break
           else:
               mu*=0.75; noise*=0.85
       if not ok:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 200:
             # kick
             p=best.copy()
             p=(1-0.3)*p +0.3*random_smooth(n,rng)
             p=safe_normalize(p); q=conv_square(p); cur=score
             no_improve=0; mu=0.25; noise=0.05
          else:
             mu*=0.9
       if time-last_print>5: print...
    return best,best_score
```
`mult_step` uses `project_weights`.

`random_smooth(n,rng)` top-level:
```
def random_smooth(n, rng, order=5):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   y=np.zeros(n)
   for k in range(1,order+1):
      amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.8)
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
      y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x/n + phase)
   a=np.exp(y - np.max(y))
   a += 1e-4
   return a/a.sum()
```
No lambda.

`mult_step`:
```
def mult_step(p,q,power,target_score,n,mu,noise,rng):
   qmax=q.max()
   if qmax <=0: return None
   t=q/qmax
   # t may have nan? clip
   t=np.clip(t,0,1)
   wb=np.power(t,power)
   target_c=target_score/(2.0*n)
   wt=None
   if target_c < qmax:
      r=q-target_c
      r=np.where(r>0,r,0)
      rmax=r.max()
      if rmax>0:
         wr=np.power(r/rmax,power)
         wt=wr
   if wt is None:
      w=0.8*wb + 0.2/ q.size
   else:
      w=0.2*wb + 0.8*wt
   w=w+1e-6
   s=w.sum()
   if not finite or s==0: w=np.full(q.size,1/q.size)
   else: w/=s
   S=weighted_correlate(p,w)
   avg=S.mean()
   if avg <=0: return None
   H=S/avg -1
   H=np.clip(H,-6,6)
   H=H-H.mean()
   if noise>0:
       H = H + rng.normal(0.0, noise, size=n)
       H=H-H.mean()
   # To avoid extreme, maybe scale H by std: H/= (std+eps)
   std=H.std()
   if std>0: H=H/std
   newp=p*np.exp(-mu*H)
   rho=0.003
   newp=(1-rho)*newp + rho/n
   newp=safe_normalize(newp)
   return newp
```
Scaling H by std makes mu independent of weight shape. Good. But if Hstd tiny, divide large; use if >1e-8 else 0. `mu` then log change = mu*std normalized =mu. Good.

One issue: `H = S/avg - 1` then center after clip. If H std small, dividing by std increases variations if near constant? If std near0, no update. We'll do:
```
std=H.std()
if std < 1e-8: return p.copy()? maybe no change
H = H/std
```
Then noise std relative. `mu` can be 0.1. Good.

Weights `0.2*wb +0.8*wt`: If target_c close to qmax, wr sparse; if current much above target, wr shape. Good. If q values below target_c all (score below target), wt none, use wb.

`weighted_correlate` uses `N=next_fft_len(3*n-2)`, but for n=1 maybe issue; n>32. Good.

```
def beta_seq(n,p,q):
```

Grid generation function returns top sequences:
```
def generate_starts(n, max_starts, seed):
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   seqs=[]
   # uniform
   seqs.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   # height? outside
   # beta grid
   for p in [0,0.5,1,1.5,2,3]:
      for q in [0,0.5,1,1.5,2]:
          seqs.append(beta_seq(n,p,q))
   # geometric
   for alpha in [0.5,1,2,4,8]:
       seqs.append(geom); seqs.append(reverse)
   # poly: a1,a2 in [-2,0,2]
   for a1 in [-2,0,2,4]:
      for a2 in [-2,0,2]:
          seqs.append(poly)
   # random smooth 24
   for _ in range(24): seqs.append(random_smooth)
   # mixed maybe 20 random or grid p,q,t
   for p in [0,1,2]: for q in [0,1,2]: for t in [0.25,0.5,0.75]: seqs.append(mixed)
   # score, sort, top
   scored=[]
   for a in seqs:
       a=safe_normalize(a)
       q=conv_square(a); sc=score...
       if finite: scored.append((sc,a))
   scored.sort()
   # deduplicate? maybe if scores close, keep diverse. Simple: take top max_starts.
   return beginnings
```

Add height sequence:

Implement `run_spectral` with a limit on iterations and time prints.
```
def run_spectral(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0, damp=0.7):
    p=safe_normalize(a0)
    n=p.size
    q=conv_square(p); cur=score_from_q(q,n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
    if n<=1 or time beyond: return best,best_score
    L=2*n-1; N=next_fft_len(L)
    avg=1.0/L
    target_m=target_score/(2.0*n)
    target_m=max(target_m, avg*1.02)
    m=cur/(2.0*n)
    if m > target_m:
       M=max(target_m, 0.97*m)
    else:
       M=max(avg*1.02, 0.9*m)
    M=max(M, avg*1.02)
    it=0; no=0; last_print=...
    t0=...
    while time < deadline:
       newp=spectral_step(p,M,N,damp)
       qn=conv_square(newp); scn=score...; mn=scn/(2n)
       if scn < cur*(1-1e-9):
           p=newp; cur=scn; m=mn; it+=1; no=0
           if cur<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
           if m > target_m:
              M=max(target_m, 0.985*m)
           else:
              M=max(avg*1.02, 0.9*m)
           # adjust damp? increase damp toward 0.9 as improvement
       else:
           no+=1
           if no>15:
              # relax M but not above current
              M = min(m, max(M*1.05, target_m + 0.1*(m-target_m) if m>target_m else avg*1.05))
              if M >= m: M=max(avg*1.05, 0.97*m)
              no=0
           if no>200:
              p=best.copy(); cur=best_score; m=cur/(2n)
              M=max(avg*1.05, min(0.9*m, target_m+0.05*n?))
              no=0
              # maybe change damp sign?
       if time-last_print>5: print...
    return best,best_score
```

Let's draft code mentally:

```
"""
Search for nonnegative step heights minimizing 2n max(a*a)/(sum a)^2.
Uses FFT-based scoring and two complementary local searches:
1. spectral factor box projection (forces convolution coeffs to a cap).
2. multiplicative log-sum/sub-gradient descent with active-target weights.
Multiple parameterized/random starts, resampling, and length increases.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
try:
   from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
except Exception:
   from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft
```

`next_fft_len`:
```
def next_fft_len(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    n=1
    while n < m:
        n <<=1
    return n
```

`safe_normalize`:
```
def safe_normalize(a, floor=1e-14):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1:
        a=a.ravel()
    if a.size ==0:
        a=np.ones(1,dtype=np.float64)
    a=a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a<0.0] = 0.0
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a.fill(1.0)
        s=float(a.size)
    a=a/s
    a=np.maximum(a, floor)
    a=a/a.sum()
    return a
```

`resample_sequence`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.ndim!=1: a=a.ravel()
    if m<=0: return np.ones(1)
    a=a[ np.isfinite(a) ]
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(m)
    if a.size==1:
       if a[0]>0: return np.full(m, 1.0/m? Wait safe_normalize later. Return np.full(m,1.0)
       return np.ones(m)
    # interp coordinates 0..a.size-1 to 0..m-1
    xp=np.arange(a.size, dtype=float)
    fp=np.arange(m,dtype=float)
    y=np.interp(fp,xp,a)
    return safe_normalize(y)
```
If a.size==1 and value maybe zero, ones fine.

`conv_square`:
```
def conv_square(p):
    p=safe_normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_fft_len(L)
    A=rfft(p,N)
    q=irfft(A*A,N)
    q=q[:L]
    q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
    s=float(q.sum())
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
        q=q/s
    else:
        # fallback approximate triangular? but should not
        q=np.zeros(L); idx=(n-1); q[idx]=L? 
    return q
```
If q sum zero? impossible. Fallback uniform? `q=np.full(L,1/L)`.

`score_from_q`.

`box_sum` top-level:
```
def box_sum(c,lam,M):
   return float(np.clip(c+lam,0.0,M).sum())
```

`project_box_sum`:
```
def project_box_sum(c,M):
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
   L=c.size
   s=float(c.sum())
   if s <=0:
       d=np.full(L,1.0/L)
       return d
   c=c/s
   if M <= 0: M=1.0/(L-1) if L>1 else 1
   avg=1.0/L
   if M <= avg:
      M=avg*1.0001
   if float(c.max()) <= M*(1+1e-12):
      return c
   lo=0.0
   hi=M - float(c.min())
   if hi <= 0: hi=1e-12
   # ensure f(hi)>=1
   for _ in range(10):
       if box_sum(c,hi,M) >= 1.0:
           break
       hi *= 2.0
   for _ in range(40):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       if box_sum(c,mid,M) < 1.0:
           lo=mid
       else:
           hi=mid
   lam=(lo+hi)*0.5
   d=np.clip(c+lam,0.0,M)
   sd=float(d.sum())
   if sd <=0:
       return np.full(L,1.0/L)
   d=d/sd
   d=np.minimum(d,M)
   sd=float(d.sum())
   if sd <=0: return np.full(L,1/L)
   d=d/sd
   return d
```
After final normalize, due sum maybe slightly less if clipped, max might exceed M by tiny. Good.

`spectral_step` as above.

After d project, `Ctar = rfft(d, N)`. `Ctar` complex. If N even, set Nyquist abs real:
```
if N % 2 == 0:
    yq = float(np.real(Ctar[N//2]))
    Ctar[N//2] = abs(yq) + 0j
```
But `rfft` output length N//2+1, index N//2 valid.
`absC = np.abs(Ctar)`. `r_target = np.sqrt(absC)`.
`A2 = np.empty_like(Ctar, dtype=complex)`. For small? Compute.
```
absA=np.abs(A)
target_phase=np.angle(Ctar)
current_phase=np.angle(A)
delta=np.angle(np.exp(1j*(target_phase - 2.0*current_phase)))
scale=r_target / np.maximum(absA, 1e-15)
scale=np.clip(scale,1e-6,1e6)
A2 = A * (scale * np.exp(1j * phase_rate * delta))
small = absA < 1e-10
A2[small] = np.sqrt(Ctar[small])
A2[0] = r_target[0] + 0j
if N%2==0: A2[N//2] = r_target[N//2] + 0j
a_new = irfft(A2, N)
a_new = a_new[:n]
a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
new=(1-damp)*a + damp*a_new
new=safe_normalize(new)
return new
```

`random_smooth`:
```
def random_smooth(n,rng,order=6):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.zeros(n)
    for k in range(1,order+1):
        amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.9))
        ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi))
        y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + ph)
    a=np.exp(y - np.max(y))
    a += 1e-3
    return safe_normalize(a)
```
Could use x*(n-1) not normalized k? fine.

Now, let's write `run_mult` code fully:
```
def run_mult(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0):
    p=safe_normalize(a0)
    n=p.size
    q=conv_square(p); cur=score_from_q(q,n)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=cur
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    mu=0.12; noise=0.02; no=0; t0=time.monotonic(); last=t0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem_total = max(1e-6, deadline - t0)
        frac = (time.monotonic() - t0) / rem_total
        if frac < 0.33: power=1.5
        elif frac < 0.66: power=2.5
        else: power=4.0
        ok=False
        for attempt in range(4):
            newp=mult_step(p,q,power,target_score,n,mu,noise,rng)
            if newp is None:
                mu *= 0.7
                noise *= 0.7
                continue
            newq=conv_square(newp)
            news=score_from_q(newq,n)
            if news < cur - 1e-12:
                p=newp; q=newq; cur=news; no=0; ok=True
                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score=cur; best=p.copy()
                if attempt > 0:
                    mu=max(0.12, mu*1.0)
                else:
                    mu=min(0.3, mu*1.02)
                noise=min(0.05, noise*1.01)
                break
            else:
                mu *= 0.75
                noise *= 0.85
        if not ok:
            no +=1
            mu *= 0.95
            if no > 250 or (no > 100 and time.monotonic()-last > 20):
                p=best.copy()
                # kick
                kick=random_smooth(n, rng)
                p=(0.85*best + 0.15*kick)
                p=safe_normalize(p)
                q=conv_square(p); cur=score_from_q(q,n)
                no=0; mu=0.2; noise=0.04
        if time.monotonic() - last > 10:
            last=time.monotonic()
            print(f"mult n={n} best={best_score:.7f} cur={cur:.7f}", flush=True)
    return best,best_score
```
The kick condition `time-last>20` might restart too often; use `if no > 250` only. Good.

Potential improvement: If current local best much worse than global? We don't pass global. It only kicks to local best. Main later runs global best. OK.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total_budget=float(budget_s)
    deadline=t0 + max(5.0, total_budget - 20.0)
    # choose n
    if total_budget >= 350: n_main=4096
    elif total_budget >= 180: n_main=2048
    else: n_main=1024
    target_score=1.49
    print("...")
    grid_deadline=min(deadline, t0+max(5, total_budget*0.06))
    top=generate_grid_starts(n_main, seed)
    # top list tuples (score, seq)
    ordered=[]
    weights=[5,4,3,2,2,1,1,1,1,1]
    for i,(sc,seq) in enumerate(top):
       if i>=10: break
       ordered.append((seq, weights[i] if i<len(weights) else 1))
    h=get_height_sequence()
    if h is not None:
       if h.size > 20:
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

The implementation uses FFT-based surrogate scoring and three complementary
heuristics:
  - spectral box-projection: cap the convolution coefficients and take a
    damped spectral square-root factor,
  - multiplicative subgradient/log-sum descent on active or target-exceeding
    convolution coefficients,
  - diverse starts from named parameterized families, random smooth starts,
    and the supplied height_sequence_1 when available.
Finally, the best candidates are verified with the exact evaluator.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from operator import itemgetter

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import rfft, irfft


def next_fft_len(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def safe_normalize(a, floor=1e-14):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a[a < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a.fill(1.0)
        s = float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def resample_sequence(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if m <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a[np.isfinite(a)]
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    xp = np.arange(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    xp_new = np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.interp(xp_new, xp, a)
    return safe_normalize(y)


def conv_square_no_clean(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_fft_len(L)
    A = rfft(p, N)
    q = irfft(A * A, N)
    q = q[:L]
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        q = q / s
    else:
        q = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return q


def conv_square(p):
    p = safe_normalize(p)
    return conv_square_no_clean(p)


def score_from_q(q, n):
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(q))


def exact_score(seq):
    try:
        return float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def box_sum(c, lam, M):
    return float(np.clip(c + lam, 0.0, M).sum())


def project_box_sum(c, M):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    L = int(c.size)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if L <= 0 or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) if L > 0 else np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    c = c / s
    avg = 1.0 / L
    if M is None or not np.isfinite(M) or M <= avg:
        M = avg * 1.0001
    if float(np.max(c)) <= M * (1.0 + 1e-12):
        return c

    lo = 0.0
    hi = M - float(np.min(c))
    if hi <= 1e-15:
        hi = 1e-12

    for _ in range(12):
        if box_sum(c, hi, M) >= 1.0:
            break
        hi *= 2.0

    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        if box_sum(c, mid, M) < 1.0:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    d = np.clip(c + lam, 0.0, M)
    sd = float(d.sum())
    if sd <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    d = d / sd
    d = np.clip(d, 0.0, M)
    sd = float(d.sum())
    if sd <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return d / sd


def spectral_step(a, M, N, damp=0.65, phase_rate=0.9):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = rfft(a, N)
    Ccur = A * A
    c = irfft(Ccur, N)
    c = c[:L]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(c.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        c = c / s
    d = project_box_sum(c, M)
    Ctar = rfft(d, N)

    if N % 2 == 0:
        yq = float(np.real(Ctar[N // 2]))
        Ctar[N // 2] = abs(yq) + 0.0j

    absC = np.abs(Ctar)
    r_target = np.sqrt(absC)
    absA = np.abs(A)

    target_phase = np.angle(Ctar)
    current_phase = np.angle(A)
    delta = np.angle(np.exp(1j * (target_phase - 2.0 * current_phase)))

    scale = r_target / np.maximum(absA, 1e-15)
    scale = np.clip(scale, 1e-6, 1e6)
    A2 = A * (scale * np.exp(1j * phase_rate * delta))

    small = absA < 1e-10
    if np.any(small):
        A2[small] = np.sqrt(Ctar[small])

    A2 = np.nan_to_num(A2, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    if r_target.size > 0:
        A2[0] = r_target[0] + 0.0j
    if N % 2 == 0 and r_target.size > N // 2:
        A2[N // 2] = r_target[N // 2] + 0.0j

    a2 = irfft(A2, N)
    a2 = a2[:n]
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)

    newa = (1.0 - damp) * a + damp * a2
    return safe_normalize(newa)


def run_spectral(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0):
    p = safe_normalize(a0)
    n = int(p.size)
    q = conv_square_no_clean(p)
    cur = score_from_q(q, n)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = cur

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best, best_score

    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_fft_len(L)
    avg = 1.0 / L
    target_m = max(float(target_score) / (2.0 * n), avg * 1.02)
    m = cur / (2.0 * n)
    M = max(target_m, 0.965 * m)
    M = max(M, avg * 1.02)

    damp = 0.65
    phase_rate = 0.9
    no_improve = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    last_print = t0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if no_improve and no_improve % 80 == 0:
            damp = max(0.25, damp * 0.92)
            phase_rate = min(1.0, phase_rate * 1.02)

        np_ = spectral_step(p, M, N, damp, phase_rate)
        q2 = conv_square_no_clean(np_)
        sc2 = score_from_q(q2, n)
        m2 = sc2 / (2.0 * n)

        if sc2 < cur - 1e-12:
            p = np_
            q = q2
            cur = sc2
            m = m2
            no_improve = 0
            damp = min(0.9, damp * 1.02)

            if m > target_m:
                M = max(target_m, 0.987 * m)
            else:
                M = max(avg * 1.02, 0.90 * m)

            if cur < best_score:
                best_score = cur
                best = p.copy()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 25:
                M_cand = max(M * 1.10, 0.995 * m)
                M = min(m, M_cand)
                if M >= 0.999 * m:
                    M = max(avg * 1.05, 0.96 * m)
                no_improve = 0
            if no_improve > 220:
                p = best.copy()
                q = conv_square_no_clean(p)
                cur = best_score
                m = cur / (2.0 * n)
                M = max(target_m, 0.94 * m)
                no_improve = 0
                damp = max(0.35, damp * 0.85)

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0:
            last_print = time.monotonic()
            print(
                f"spectral n={n} best={best_score:.7f} cur={cur:.7f} M={M:.3e}",
                flush=True,
            )

    return best, best_score


def weighted_correlate(p, w):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float((w.sum()) * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)

    L = 2 * n - 1
    total = L + n - 1
    N = next_fft_len(total)

    v = w[::-1]
    P = rfft(p, N)
    V = rfft(v, N)
    c = irfft(V * P, N)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c.real)

    start = L - 1
    stop = L - n - 1
    S = c[start:stop:-1]
    S = np.maximum(S, 0.0)
    return S


def random_smooth(n, rng, order=6):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(1, order + 1):
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.9))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        y += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
    a = np.exp(y - np.max(y))
    a += 1e-3
    return safe_normalize(a)


def mult_step(p, q, power, target_score, n, mu, noise, rng):
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if qmax <= 0.0:
        return None

    t = np.clip(q / qmax, 0.0, 1.0)
    wb = np.power(t, power)

    target_c = float(target_score) / (2.0 * n)
    wt = None
    if target_c < qmax:
        r = q - target_c
        r = np.where(r > 0.0, r, 0.0)
        rmax = float(np.max(r))
        if rmax > 0.0:
            wr = np.power(r / rmax, power)
            wt = wr

    if wt is None:
        w = 0.8 * wb + 0.2 * (1.0 / q.size)
    else:
        w = 0.25 * wb + 0.75 * wt

    w = w + 1e-8
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(q.size, 1.0 / q.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        w = w / ws

    S = weighted_correlate(p, w)
    avg = float(S.mean())
    if avg <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(avg):
        return None

    H = S / avg - 1.0
    H = np.clip(H, -6.0, 6.0)
    H = H - H.mean()
    std = float(H.std())
    if std < 1e-8:
        return None
    H = H / std

    if noise > 0.0:
        H = H + rng.normal(0.0, noise, size=n)

    newp = p * np.exp(-mu * H)
    rho = 0.003
    newp = (1.0 - rho) * newp + rho * (1.0 / n)
    return safe_normalize(newp)


def run_mult(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0):
    p = safe_normalize(a0)
    n = int(p.size)
    q = conv_square_no_clean(p)
    cur = score_from_q(q, n)
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = cur

    if n < 2 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return best, best_score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    mu = 0.12
    noise = 0.02
    no_improve = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    last_print = t0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        total = max(1e-9, deadline - t0)
        frac = (time.monotonic() - t0) / total
        if frac < 0.33:
            power = 1.6
        elif frac < 0.66:
            power = 2.6
        else:
            power = 4.2

        accepted = False
        for attempt in range(4):
            np_ = mult_step(p, q, power, target_score, n, mu, noise, rng)
            if np_ is None:
                mu *= 0.7
                noise *= 0.7
                continue
            q2 = conv_square_no_clean(np_)
            sc2 = score_from_q(q2, n)
            if sc2 < cur - 1e-12:
                p = np_
                q = q2
                cur = sc2
                no_improve = 0
                accepted = True

                if cur < best_score:
                    best_score = cur
                    best = p.copy()

                if attempt > 0:
                    mu = max(0.12, mu)
                else:
                    mu = min(0.30, mu * 1.03)
                noise = min(0.05, noise * 1.01)
                break
            else:
                mu *= 0.75
                noise *= 0.85

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            mu *= 0.95
            if no_improve > 250:
                kick = random_smooth(n, rng)
                p = 0.82 * best + 0.18 * kick
                p = safe_normalize(p)
                q = conv_square_no_clean(p)
                cur = score_from_q(q, n)
                no_improve = 0
                mu = 0.20
                noise = 0.05

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > 12.0:
            last_print = time.monotonic()
            print(
                f"mult n={n} best={best_score:.7f} cur={cur:.7f} mu={mu:.3f}",
                flush=True,
            )

    return best, best_score


def run_combined(a0, deadline, target_score, seed=0):
    p = safe_normalize(a0)
    q = conv_square_no_clean(p)
    cur = score_from_q(q, p.size)

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return p, cur

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 35.0:
        spec_end = time.monotonic() + max(6.0, 0.45 * rem)
        p1, sc1 = run_spectral(p, spec_end, target_score, seed)
    else:
        p1, sc1 = p, cur

    best_seq = p1
    best_sc = sc1

    p2, sc2 = run_mult(p1, deadline, target_score, seed + 1)
    if sc2 < best_sc:
        best_seq = p2
        best_sc = sc2

    if cur < best_sc:
        best_seq = p
        best_sc = cur

    return best_seq, best_sc


def make_beta(n, p, q):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    xi = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    a = np.power(xi, p) * np.power(1.0 - xi, q)
    return safe_normalize(a)


def make_geometric(n, alpha):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    a = np.exp(-alpha * x)
    return safe_normalize(a)


def make_poly_exp(n, a1, a2):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = a1 * x + a2 * x * x
    y = np.clip(y, -50.0, 50.0)
    return safe_normalize(np.exp(y))


def make_mixed(n, p, q, t):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    xi = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
    a = (1.0 - t) * np.power(xi, p) + t * np.power(1.0 - xi, q)
    return safe_normalize(a)


def generate_grid_starts(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    seqs = []

    seqs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for pval in (0.0, 0.35, 0.7, 1.0, 1.4, 1.9, 2.6, 3.5):
        for qval in (0.0, 0.25, 0.6, 1.0, 1.5, 2.2):
            seqs.append(make_beta(n, pval, qval))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.3, 2.2, 3.8, 7.0):
        g = make_geometric(n, alpha)
        seqs.append(g)
        seqs.append(g[::-1].copy())

    for a1 in (-3.0, -1.5, 0.0, 1.5, 3.0, 5.0):
        for a2 in (-3.0, -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.5):
            seqs.append(make_poly_exp(n, a1, a2))

    for pval in (0.0, 0.8, 1.7):
        for qval in (0.0, 0.9, 2.0):
            for tval in (0.2, 0.45, 0.65, 0.9):
                seqs.append(make_mixed(n, pval, qval, tval))

    for _ in range(28):
        seqs.append(random_smooth(n, rng))

    scored = []
    for a in seqs:
        a = safe_normalize(a)
        q = conv_square_no_clean(a)
        sc = score_from_q(q, a.size)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, a))

    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    return scored


def get_height_sequence():
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        return None
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    if h.size == 0 or float(h.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    h = h / float(h.sum())
    return h


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(max(10.0, float(budget_s)))
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, total_budget - 20.0)
    target_score = 1.49

    if total_budget >= 350.0:
        n_main = 4096
    elif total_budget >= 180.0:
        n_main = 2048
    else:
        n_main = 1024

    print(f"start budget={total_budget:.1f}s n_main={n_main} target={target_score:.4f}", flush=True)

    gen_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + max(5.0, total_budget * 0.055))
    top_starts = generate_grid_starts(n_main, int(seed))
    if not top_starts:
        top_starts = [(2.0, safe_normalize(np.ones(n_main)))]

    best_seq = top_starts[0][1].copy()
    best_score = float(top_starts[0][0])
    print(f"initial grid best={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    hseq = get_height_sequence()
    if hseq is not None:
        qh = conv_square_no_clean(hseq)
        shr = score_from_q(qh, hseq.size)
        if shr < best_score:
            best_score = shr
            best_seq = hseq.copy()

    ordered = []
    weights = [5, 4, 3, 2, 2, 1, 1, 1, 1, 1]
    for i, (sc, seq) in enumerate(top_starts[:10]):
        w = weights[i] if i < len(weights) else 1
        ordered.append((seq, w))

    if hseq is not None and hseq.size > 20:
        hr = resample_sequence(hseq, n_main)
        ordered.insert(1, (hr, 4))
        if hseq.size <= 10000:
            ordered.append((safe_normalize(hseq), 2))

    rng0 = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 12345)
    ordered.append((random_smooth(n_main, rng0), 1))

    i = 0
    while i < len(ordered) and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = deadline - 20.0 - now
        if rem < 8.0:
            break
        total_w = 0
        for j in range(i, len(ordered)):
            total_w += int(ordered[j][1])
        if total_w <= 0:
            break
        w_i = int(ordered[i][1])
        alloc = rem * (float(w_i) / float(total_w))
        alloc = min(alloc, 0.6 * rem)
        if alloc < 8.0:
            break
        end = now + alloc

        seq0, _ = ordered[i]
        p1, sc1 = run_combined(seq0, end, target_score, int(seed) + 100 * i + 7)
        if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc1)
            best_seq = np.asarray(p1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"after start {i}: best={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

        i += 1

    large_seq = None
    large_score = float("inf")

    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        rem = deadline - 15.0 - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(70.0, 0.35 * rem)
        if alloc >= 5.0:
            end = time.monotonic() + alloc
            p1, sc1 = run_combined(best_seq, end, target_score, int(seed) + 5555)
            if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc1)
                best_seq = np.asarray(p1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print(f"after long best refinement: best={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
        n_large = int(min(8192, max(2 * n_main, int(best_seq.size))))
        if int(best_seq.size) < n_large:
            large0 = resample_sequence(best_seq, n_large)
        else:
            large0 = safe_normalize(best_seq)
        rem = deadline - 15.0 - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(110.0, 0.75 * rem)
        if alloc >= 10.0:
            end = time.monotonic() + alloc
            p1, sc1 = run_combined(large0, end, target_score, int(seed) + 7777)
            if np.isfinite(sc1):
                large_seq = np.asarray(p1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                large_score = float(sc1)
                if large_score < best_score:
                    best_score = large_score
                    best_seq = large_seq.copy()
                    print(f"after larger length: best={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)

    if hseq is not None and 500 <= hseq.size <= 10000 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
        rem = deadline - 10.0 - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(35.0, 0.9 * rem)
        if alloc >= 5.0:
            end = time.monotonic() + alloc
            p1, sc1 = run_combined(hseq, end, target_score, int(seed) + 9999)
            if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc1)
                best_seq = np.asarray(p1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                print(f"after original-height refinement: best={best_score:.7f}", flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < large_score:
                large_score = float(sc1)
                large_seq = np.asarray(p1, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    candidates = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        candidates.append((best_score, best_seq))
    if large_seq is not None and large_score < float("inf"):
        candidates.append((large_score, large_seq))
    if top_starts:
        candidates.append((top_starts[0][0], top_starts[0][1]))
    if hseq is not None:
        candidates.append((score_from_q(conv_square_no_clean(hseq), hseq.size), hseq))

    candidates.sort(key=itemgetter(0))

    final_seq = best_seq if best_seq is not None else (candidates[0][1] if candidates else np.ones(1024))
    final_score = float("inf")

    seen = set()
    checked = 0
    for sc, seq in candidates:
        if checked >= 5:
            break
        arr = safe_normalize(seq)
        key = tid = id(arr)
        if tid in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(tid)
        checked += 1
        try:
            val = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr]))
        except Exception:
            val = float("inf")
        print(f"exact eval candidate fft~{sc:.7f} exact={val:.7f} n={arr.size}", flush=True)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < final_score:
            final_score = val
            final_seq = arr

    if final_seq is None or not np.isfinite(final_score):
        try:
            h = get_height_sequence()
            if h is not None:
                final_seq = safe_normalize(h)
                final_score = exact_score([float(x) for x in final_seq])
            else:
                final_seq = safe_normalize(np.ones(1024))
                final_score = exact_score([float(x) for x in final_seq])
        except Exception:
            final_seq = safe_normalize(np.ones(1024))
            final_score = float("nan")

    final_seq = np.asarray(final_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final_seq[~np.isfinite(final_seq)] = 0.0
    final_seq = np.clip(final_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(final_seq.sum()) < 0.01:
        final_seq = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    print(f"final returned n={final_seq.size} exact_score={final_score:.7f}", flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in final_seq]
```
